Based on these criteria, the framework progressively introduces challenging samples during training, enabling models to learn robust representations in a coarse-to-fine manner. We evaluate our method on the challenging SeracFallDet benchmark, where results demonstrate consistent improvements of the proposed approach over standard uniform-sampling strategies for both pixel-based and object-based approaches. These results highlight the potential of curriculum learning to improve robustness in deep change detection. Importantly, our training framework is orthogonal to existing CD architectures, making it readily applicable to a broad range of methods.

</details>


### [70] [TempAct: Advancing Temporal Plausibility in Autoregressive Video Generation via Planner-Executor RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28016)
*Jing Wang,Xiangxin Zhou,Jiajun Liang,Kaiqi Liu,Wanyun Pang,Zhenyu Xie,Tianyu Pang,Xiaodan Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Autoregressive (AR) video diffusion models enable low-latency streaming generation by synthesizing videos chunk by chunk with cached visual context, but this chunk-wise formulation makes temporal instruction following ambiguous. A single global prompt does not specify which sub-event should be realized in each chunk, while naively switching to step-wise prompts often leads to delayed reactions, blended step semantics, and error propagation across prompt transitions. These failures are difficult to address with supervised fine-tuning or distillation alone: SFT suffers from exposure bias, while rollout-based distillation still optimizes low-level denoising or teacher-distribution matching rather than directly enforcing action ordering and prompt-transition correctness. We address these challenges with TempAct, a planner--executor reinforcement learning framework that jointly optimizes temporal decomposition and step-conditioned execution for temporally plausible AR video generation. TempAct uses an LLM planner to explore span-aware step prompts that are executable by the video model, and trains an AR diffusion executor to follow these prompts under its own generated histories. Its key mechanism is hierarchical group exploration: candidate plans form planning groups, and each plan induces an execution group of multiple continuations from a shared visual context, enabling plan-level credit assignment for long-horizon temporal outcomes and executor-level credit assignment for prompt-switch behavior. We further design hierarchical rewards that combine plan-quality and full-video temporal feedback for the planner with local transition-level step-following rewards, aesthetic regularization, and KL constraints for the executor. Experiments on Self-Forcing and LongLive show that TempAct improves temporal consistency while preserving overall visual quality.

</details>


### [71] [EMOSH: Expressive Motion and Shape Disentanglement for Human Animation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28026)
*Dongbin Zhang,Hao Liu,Binquan Dai,Kangjie Chen,Chuming Wang,Chen Li,Jing Lyu,Haoqian Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EMOSH是一个新颖的高保真可控人体视频生成框架，它通过引入表达性人体模型（EHM）和由粗到精的混合运动注入策略，在从根本上解决身形泄漏问题的同时，实现了对生动面部表情和复杂手势的精细控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可控人体动画方法在表达性与解纠缠性之间存在困境：主流的2D姿态条件方法存在“运动-形状纠缠”，导致驱动对象的身形泄漏到生成结果中；而依赖3D先验（如SMPL）的方法虽能实现几何解纠缠，却难以准确捕捉面部表情和复杂手势，导致动画效果生硬、缺乏表现力。

Method: 本文提出了EMOSH框架。首先，引入了表达性人体模型（EHM）作为核心控制表示，通过显式解纠缠形状与姿态参数，从根本上解决身形泄漏问题，并设计了一个鲁棒的运动跟踪器来从视频中准确估计EHM参数。其次，提出了一种由粗到精的混合运动注入策略，以实现对表情和手势的更精细控制。最后，引入了空间对齐条件机制，以弥合训练与推理之间的域差异，提升生成视频的身份一致性。

Result: 大量实验表明，EMOSH在自驱动和跨驱动场景中均优于现有的方法，能够生成高保真度、表情生动逼真的视频，同时有效维持了形状的解纠缠，避免了身形泄漏问题。

Conclusion: EMOSH框架通过创新的EHM表示和混合控制策略，成功解决了可控人体动画生成中表达性与解纠缠性难以兼得的核心问题。该方法在生成高保真、强表现力的人体视频方面展现出显著优势，对内容创作和数字人应用具有重要价值。

Abstract: High-fidelity and expressive controllable human animation is essential for content creation and digital avatar applications. However, existing methods face a dilemma between expressiveness and disentanglement. Mainstream 2D pose-conditioned approaches suffer from "motion-shape entanglement", leading to the leakage of the driving subject's body shape. Conversely, methods relying on 3D priors (e.g., SMPL) achieve geometric disentanglement but struggle to capture facial expressions and complex gestures, resulting in rigid animations. To this end, we propose EMOSH, a novel framework for high-fidelity controllable human video generation. First, an Expressive Human Model (EHM) is introduced as the core control representation. By explicitly disentangling shape and pose parameters, we fundamentally resolve the body shape leakage issue. Alongside this, a robust motion tracker is designed to accurately estimate EHM parameters from video. Second, we propose a Coarse-to-Fine Hybrid Motion Injection strategy, enabling more fine-grained control over expressions and gestures. Furthermore, we introduce a Spatially-Aligned Conditioning mechanism to bridge the domain gap between training and inference, improving identity consistency. Extensive experiments demonstrate that EMOSH outperforms previous methods in both self-driven and cross-driven scenarios, producing high-fidelity videos with vivid expressions while maintaining shape disentanglement.

</details>


### [72] [Mind the Gap: Quantifying the Domain Gap in Cross-Sensor Diffusion Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28039)
*Dawid Kopeć,Katarzyna Jabłońska,Wojciech Kozłowski,Maciej Zięba*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究首次系统分析了合成数据与真实跨传感器卫星图像之间的域差距对扩散模型超分辨率性能的影响，揭示了当前方法的关键局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率卫星图像需求增长，超分辨率技术用于弥合Sentinel-2等免费传感器与PlanetScope等商业系统之间的空间分辨率差距。现有模型通常在合成降质数据上训练，导致在真实跨传感器图像上存在域差距，影响实际应用效果。

Method: 1. 使用Sentinel-2和PlanetScope之间几何和时间对齐的大规模数据集；2. 在控制实验环境下评估五种先进扩散架构；3. 提出基于Sentinel-2自监督特征的领域自适应感知指标LPIPS-Sat。

Result: 1. 合成数据训练的模型在真实数据对上性能急剧下降；2. 真实跨传感器数据训练的模型存在优化困难，难以适应物理和辐射测量多样性。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了当前超分辨率技术的一个关键局限：需要发展能够将超分辨率与域适应解耦的新方法，以应对合成与真实数据之间的系统性差异。

Abstract: Demand for high-resolution satellite imagery has increased interest in super-resolution (SR) to bridge the spatial resolution gap between freely available missions such as Sentinel-2 and commercial systems like PlanetScope. Because no sensor provides true paired low- and high-resolution observations, SR models are usually trained on synthetically degraded data, creating a domain gap on real cross-sensor imagery. In this work, we provide the first systematic study of how this synthetic-to-real mismatch affects the performance of modern diffusion-based SR models. Using a large, geometrically and temporally aligned dataset of Sentinel-2 and PlanetScope imagery, we evaluate five state-of-the-art diffusion architectures under controlled experimental settings. We also introduce LPIPS-Sat, a domain-adapted perceptual metric based on Sentinel-2 self-supervised features. Our results show two persistent challenges: synthetically trained models degrade sharply on real pairs, while models trained on real cross-sensor data exhibit optimisation difficulties and struggle to adapt to the physical and radiometric diversity. These findings highlight a key limitation of current SR and motivate methods that disentangle super-resolution from domain adaptation.

</details>


### [73] [AirGroundBench: Probing Spatial Intelligence in Multimodal Large Models under Heterogeneous Multi-View Embodied Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28049)
*Haotian Li,Yida Wang,Leyuan Wang,Jinshan Lai,Keyang Wang,Zonghao Guo,Qiang Ma,Liuyu Xiang,Jianwei Hu,Zhaofeng He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AirGroundBench是一个用于评估多模态大语言模型在异构无人机-无人车协作场景中多视角空间智能的基准测试。它揭示了当前模型在跨视角对齐和空间变换推理方面的关键瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试主要关注单智能体、单视角感知，缺乏对空中-地面多智能体协作场景的系统评估。在该场景中，多尺度观测虽可互补，但会引入尺度失配、非对称遮挡和参考系不一致等挑战。

Method: 研究构建了AirGroundBench基准测试，包含11个高保真仿真环境、1021对同步空地观测数据、约6.2万个双视角单选题和115个闭环视觉语言导航任务。测试覆盖10种任务类型，涵盖空间感知、跨视角对齐、空间变换与推理、具身决策四个渐进式能力维度，并提供结构化的空间标注（如跨视角物体一致性和度量级2D/3D边界框）。

Result: 对13个代表性多模态大语言模型在仅无人机、仅无人车和双视角输入设置下的评估表明：模型在空间感知任务上表现相对较好，但在跨视角对齐和基于变换的推理上存在明显困难，这些缺陷进一步影响了视觉语言导航中的序列决策。尽管双视角输入相比单视角能带来一定提升，但与人类表现仍有显著差距。

Conclusion: 当前 embodied 多模态大语言模型的关键局限在于几何一致性。AirGroundBench为系统评估和提升模型在异构多智能体协作中的空间智能提供了有效工具。

Abstract: In recent years, multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have shown strong potential for embodied intelligence, yet their ability to maintain geometrically consistent spatial understanding across heterogeneous views remains under-evaluated. Existing benchmarks largely focus on single-agent, single-view perception, leaving a gap in the systematic assessment of collaborative air-ground settings, where multi-scale observations are complementary but introduce scale mismatch, asymmetric occlusion, and reference-frame inconsistencies. We present AirGroundBench, a diagnostic benchmark for evaluating multi-view spatial intelligence in heterogeneous UAV-UGV collaboration. AirGroundBench is built from 11 high-fidelity simulated environments with 1,021 synchronized air-ground observation pairs, yielding approximately 62,000 dual-view, four-option single-choice visual question answering instances and 115 closed-loop vision-language navigation episodes. It covers 10 task types organized into four progressively demanding capability dimensions: spatial perception, cross-view alignment, spatial transformation and reasoning, and embodied decision-making. To support geometry-grounded evaluation and analysis, we provide structured spatial annotations, including cross-view object identities and metric 2D and 3D bounding boxes. Evaluations of 13 representative MLLMs under UAV-only, UGV-only, and dual-view input settings reveal consistent bottlenecks: models perform relatively well on spatial perception but struggle with cross-view alignment and transformation-intensive reasoning, and these deficits propagate to sequential decision-making in vision-language navigation. Although dual-view inputs provide measurable gains over single-view variants, a persistent gap from human performance remains, highlighting geometric consistency as a key limitation of current embodied MLLMs.

</details>


### [74] [ReScene: Structured Indoor Scene Reconstruction from Multi-View Captures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28060)
*Haoran Xu,Lechao Zhang,Daoguo Dong,Yan Gao,Xin Tan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ReScene是一个从多视角捕获构建物理合理、仿真就绪3D场景的框架，它将多视角几何作为统一先验，显著提升了重建质量、渲染效果和感知一致性，并大幅提升了处理速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建仿真就绪的3D场景是具身人工智能的关键瓶颈，因为下游任务需要物体级结构、明确的物体间关系和物理合理性。现有方法存在依赖专用捕获硬件、物体重建中的单视角偏差，或布局几何合理但物理不一致等问题。作者指出，核心挑战不在于单物体重建，而在于跨视角关系融合与物理合理的场景组装。

Method: 提出ReScene框架，以多视角几何为统一先验贯穿流程。主要包括两个组件：1) HierView：基于语义一致性和3D覆盖完整性对重建视角进行优先排序，替代了混淆图像占据与物体覆盖的最大掩码启发式方法；2) 关系感知组装：将视觉语言模型的多帧关系预测与几何及房间外壳先验融合成置信度加权的场景图，以实现物理一致的场景组装。

Result: 在ScanNet场景集上，ReScene在几何、渲染和感知质量上均达到新的最先进水平。相比最强基线，Chamfer距离降低17%，LPIPS降低26%，且运行速度比先前的多视角方法快达10倍。基于重建的场景生成了具身视觉问答数据集，其上微调的Qwen-VL模型在多个空间推理任务上接近强大闭源模型的性能。

Conclusion: ReScene通过将多视角几何作为统一先验，有效解决了跨视角关系融合与物理合理场景组装的挑战，在重建质量和效率上取得了显著进步，并成功支持了下游的具身人工智能任务，如视觉问答中的空间推理。

Abstract: Constructing simulation-ready 3D scenes from multi-view captures is a key bottleneck for Embodied Artificial Intelligence, as downstream tasks require object-level structure, explicit inter-object relations, and physical plausibility. Existing approaches either rely on specialized capture hardware, suffer from single-view bias in object reconstruction, or yield layouts that are geometrically reasonable but physically inconsistent. We identify that the problem is not single-object reconstruction but cross-view relation fusion and physically plausible scene assembly. To address this challenge, we present ReScene, a framework that threads multi-view geometry throughout the pipeline as a unifying prior. Our method consists of two main components: HierView prioritizes reconstruction views based on semantic consistency and 3D coverage completeness, replacing the largest-mask heuristic that conflates image occupancy with object coverage; and Relation-Aware Assembly fuses multi-frame relation predictions from a vision-language model with geometric and room-shell priors into a confidence-weighted scene graph, enabling physically consistent scene assembly. ReScene sets a new state of the art across geometry, rendering, and perceptual quality on a set of ScanNet scenes, achieving a 17% reduction in Chamfer Distance and 26% in LPIPS over the strongest prior baseline, while running up to 10x faster than prior multi-view methods. Based on the reconstructed scenes, we also generate an embodied visual question answering dataset, on which fine-tuned Qwen-VL approaches the performance of strong closed-source models on several spatial reasoning tasks.

</details>


### [75] [TextDS: Parameter-Efficient Representation Alignment for Scene Text Detection under Distribution Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28077)
*Boyuan Chen,Zichen Dang,Chuang Yang,Lap-pui Chau,Yi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了TextDS框架，用于解决场景文本检测中的分布偏移问题。该框架采用基于视觉基础模型的双编码器设计、渐进式低秩自适应（SWLoRA）和共享子空间融合（CSF）技术，仅使用4.9M可训练参数即可在跨域和恶劣成像条件下实现稳健的检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有场景文本检测器在真实世界部署中面临训练分布之外的分布偏移问题。虽然先前的工作常依赖于大规模场景文本预训练，但在跨域变化和真实世界成像退化条件下的评估仍然有限。

Method: 1. 提出数据高效的双编码器设计，利用视觉基础模型，摆脱对大规模场景文本预训练的依赖。2. 引入逐步低秩自适应（SWLoRA），结合动态早退机制进行渐进式特征优化。3. 提出通用子空间融合（CSF），在共享子空间中对齐和融合两个分支，同时保留互补的、对分布偏移鲁棒的信息。4. 构建了恶劣条件下的场景文本检测数据集，以填补在成像退化条件下评估的空白。

Result: 实验表明，TextDS在场景文本检测中取得了有竞争力的性能，仅使用4.9M可训练参数，便在跨域和恶劣成像条件下展示了鲁棒性。

Conclusion: TextDS是一个高效且鲁棒的场景文本检测框架，通过创新的双编码器设计、SWLoRA自适应和CSF融合方法，有效解决了分布偏移问题，减少了对大规模预训练的依赖，并在恶劣成像条件下保持了良好性能。

Abstract: In real-world deployments, scene text detectors inevitably face distribution shifts beyond the training distribution. Prior work often depends on large-scale scene-text pretraining, yet evaluation under cross-domain changes and real-world imaging degradations remains limited. We propose TextDS, an efficient framework for scene text detection under distribution shifts. First, we propose a data-efficient dual-encoder design with visual foundation models, eliminating the reliance on large-scale scene-text pretraining. Second, we introduce Step-wise LoRA adaptation (SWLoRA), which performs progressive low-rank refinement with a dynamic early-exit mechanism for effective feature adaptation. Third, we propose Common Subspace Fusion (CSF) to align and fuse the two branches in a shared subspace while retaining complementary, shift-robust information. Finally, we construct adverse-condition scene text detection datasets to address the gap in evaluating under imaging degradation. Experiments show that TextDS achieves competitive performance in scene text detection, demonstrating robustness across domains and adverse imaging conditions with only 4.9M trainable parameters.

</details>


### [76] [STAG: Spatio-temporal Evolving Structural Representation of Action Units for Micro-expression Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28083)
*Nandani Sharma,Varun Sharma,Dinesh Singh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为STAG的动态ROI-AU耦合时空网络，用于解决微表情识别中因面部肌肉运动细微短暂而导致的挑战。该方法通过联合建模运动流和自适应面部连接性，改进了传统方法对顶点-起始帧的依赖，增强了跨数据集的泛化能力，并在多个数据集上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微表情识别面临的主要挑战包括：面部肌肉运动细微且短暂，现有方法过度依赖顶点-起始帧，忽略了细粒度的帧间动态信息；同时，空间和时间信息被分开建模，限制了模型在不同数据集上的泛化能力。这些因素共同导致了识别准确性和鲁棒性的不足。

Method: 本文提出了STAG框架，其核心方法包括：1. 基于幅度的选择和时间注意力机制提取光流，以捕捉判别性帧的运动信息；2. 双分支架构，结合增强图注意力网络进行结构化空间推理，并利用Transformer编码器进行时间建模；3. 双向交叉注意力模块，实现空间和时间特征的相互细化；4. AU引导的动态连接性，根据面部肌肉激活模式自适应调整区域交互；5. 使用焦点损失优化融合表示，以提升模型对难分类样本的关注。

Result: 实验在CASME II、4DME、DFME、NaME、SAMM和SMIC-HS等多个微表情数据集上进行评估。结果表明，STAG方法在鲁棒性、泛化性、可解释性和计算效率方面均优于现有方法，验证了自适应关系推理、AU引导动态连接性和深度时空特征融合在跨数据集微表情识别中的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文通过STAG网络成功解决了微表情识别中的关键挑战，证明了联合建模运动流和自适应面部连接性的重要性。该方法不仅提升了识别的准确性和跨数据集泛化能力，还增强了模型的可解释性，为微表情识别领域提供了一种有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Micro-expression recognition is challenging due to subtle and short-lived facial muscle movements. Existing methods rely heavily on apex-onset frames, overlook fine-grained inter-frame dynamics, and separately model spatial and temporal information, limiting generalization across datasets. To address these challenges, we propose STAG, a dynamic ROI-AU-coupled spatial-temporal network that jointly models motion flow and adaptive facial connectivity. The framework extracts optical flow from discriminative frames using magnitude-based selection and temporal attention. A dual-branch architecture combines an enhanced graph attention network for structured spatial reasoning with a transformer encoder for temporal modeling. A bidirectional cross-attention module enables mutual refinement of spatial and temporal features, while AU-guided dynamic connectivity adapts facial region interactions according to muscle activation patterns. The transformer captures subtle temporal dynamics beyond apex-based approaches, improving semantic consistency and interpretability for explainable micro-expression recognition. The fused representation is optimized using focal loss and evaluated on CASME II, 4DME, DFME, NaME, SAMM, and SMIC-HS. Extensive experiments demonstrate improved robustness, generalization, interpretability, and computational efficiency, confirming the effectiveness of adaptive relational reasoning, AU-guided dynamic connectivity, and deep spatial-temporal feature fusion for accurate cross-dataset micro-expression recognition.

</details>


### [77] [RPM-Distill: Physiology-guided Adaptive Cross-modal Distillation for Robust Remote Physiological Measurement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28089)
*Jiyao Wang,Qingyong Hu,Duoxun Tang,Xiao Yang,Kaishun Wu,Jiangbo Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了RPM-Distill，一个生理学引导的跨模态蒸馏框架，通过仅在训练时使用射频（RF）雷达数据来指导视频模型学习，从而提升基于视频的远程生理测量（RPM）在挑战性条件下的鲁棒性，推理时仅需视频输入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于视频的远程生理测量（RPM）虽然易用，但在不同光照、肤色和运动条件下仍然脆弱。射频（RF）雷达对光照和外观变化不敏感，能提供互补的心肺微动线索，但由于部署限制，在推理时使用雷达往往不切实际。因此，需要一种方法，在训练时利用雷达的互补优势，同时保持仅视频推理的实用性。

Method: 提出了RPM-Distill框架。其核心观察是RGB和RF波形虽然在传感物理和时域形态上不同，但在频域共享相似的潜在周期性节律。方法通过以下设计进行知识蒸馏：1）在频域对齐基本峰值；2）匹配非峰值背景分布；3）保留频谱形态和锐度。为避免因教师质量和对齐不确定性导致的负迁移，引入了一个谱策略网络，该网络从学生-教师频谱关系图中预测样本级的蒸馏门控和权重，并通过在小型带标签验证集上的元双层目标进行学习。

Result: 在挑战性条件和跨数据集的广泛实验中，RPM-Distill相比单模态基线，平均绝对误差（MAE）降低了81%，相关性提升了21%。

Conclusion: RPM-Distill框架通过在训练阶段进行有效的跨模态频域知识蒸馏，显著提升了仅基于视频的远程生理测量在复杂现实场景中的鲁棒性和准确性，证明了利用射频雷达信号作为训练时指导教师的可行性。

Abstract: Video-based remote physiological measurement (RPM) is highly accessible but remains fragile under varying illumination, skin tones, and motion. Radio frequency (RF) radar is largely invariant to illumination and appearance, providing complementary cardio-respiratory micro-motion cues; however, requiring radar at inference is often impractical due to its limited ubiquity and deployment overhead. We propose RPM-Distill, a physiology-guided cross-modal distillation framework that leverages synchronized radar only during training while retaining video-only inference. Our key observation is that although RGB and RF waveforms differ in sensing physics and time-domain morphology, they share similar latent periodic rhythm in the frequency domain. We thus distill physiology-structured spectral evidence to improve robustness, via losses that (i) anchor the fundamental peak, (ii) match the off-peak background distribution, and (iii) preserve spectral morphology and sharpness. To avoid negative transfer under sample-level teacher quality and alignment uncertainty, a spectral policy network predicts sample-level distillation gates and component weights from the student--teacher spectral relation map, learned with a meta bilevel objective on a small labeled validation split. Through extensive experiments in challenging conditions and cross-dataset settings, RPM-Distill brings 81\% MAE and 21\% correlation improvement over unimodal baselines. Code is at https://github.com/WJULYW/RPM-Distill.

</details>


### [78] [Diffusion Model Attribution via Spectral Coupling of Denoiser Responses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28092)
*Pragati Shuddhodhan Meshram,Varun Chandrasekaran*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Spectral Denoising Signatures (SDS)的非侵入性方法，用于将生成的图像准确归因到其源扩散模型。该方法通过分析模型在去噪过程中表现出的独特谱几何特征来提取稳定指纹，在跨模型和跨域场景下均实现了高准确率，为扩散模型的溯源验证提供了实用解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在生成图像领域的快速发展带来了知识产权保护和内容溯源的重要需求。现有非侵入性归因方法面临两大挑战：1）不同模型可能收敛到相似的输出分布，使得基于生成内容本身的归因不可靠；2）当模型共享相同自动编码器时，现有方法依赖的特征信号会失效。因此，需要开发一种能够克服这些限制的新型归因技术。

Method: 提出了Spectral Denoising Signatures (SDS)方法，核心思想是利用扩散模型去噪得分函数在频域表现出的独特谱几何特征。具体实现包括：1）使用频率控制的扰动来探测模型的去噪行为；2）通过分析模型在空间频率带上的能量重分布模式来提取指纹；3）仅需标准前向传播操作，无需模型反转、优化或生成时的注册步骤。

Result: 实验结果表明：1）SDS方法在八个不同的扩散模型上实现了约99.9%的归因准确率；2）在跨域提示转移的挑战性场景下，仍保持96.2%的准确率；3）在训练数据、模型架构和训练程序等各种变化条件下，均优于现有的非侵入性基线方法。

Conclusion: 谱几何为扩散模型归因提供了一个有原则且实用的基础。SDS方法通过利用模型内在的频域特征，有效解决了现有归因技术的局限性，实现了高精度、高稳定性的模型溯源。该方法不需要修改生成过程或依赖特定信号，为扩散模型的知识产权保护和内容验证提供了可靠的工具。

Abstract: Attributing a generated image to its source diffusion model is a fundamental challenge in provenance verification and intellectual property protection. This problem is particularly difficult because diffusion models trained on different datasets can converge to similar score functions and thus similar output distributions, making the generated images themselves unreliable as attribution evidence. Existing non-invasive methods either fail on architecturally similar variants or rely on signals that vanish when models share the same autoencoder. We propose Spectral Denoising Signatures (SDS), a non-invasive attribution method that identifies the source model by fingerprinting each candidate model's denoising behavior. Our key insight is that a model's denoising score function exhibits a distinctive spectral geometry, reflected in how it redistributes energy across spatial frequency bands during denoising. By probing this behavior with frequency-controlled perturbations, SDS extracts a stable signature that is intrinsic to the model, requiring only standard forward passes with no inversion, optimization, or generation-time enrollment. Our results demonstrate that SDS achieves approximately 99.9% accuracy across eight diverse diffusion models and 96.2% under cross-domain prompt shift, outperforming non-invasive baselines across variations in training data, architecture, and training procedure, establishing spectral geometry as a principled and practical basis for diffusion model attribution. Code is available at: https://github.com/Pragati-Meshram/SGS

</details>


### [79] [OSOR: One-Step Diffusion Inpainting for Effect-Aware Object Removal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28094)
*Qinming Zhou,Chenxi Sun,Deyang Kong,Junhao He,Xiangheng Tang,Peike Yu,Haotian Wu,Leilei Cao,Linfeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OSOR是一种基于扩散模型的高效对象移除方法，通过单步推理实现强大的移除效果，同时处理阴影/反射等复杂效应和不准确的掩码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界对象移除面临两大挑战：1）目标对象的非局部效应（如阴影、反射）难以建模；2）用户提供的掩码常不准确或不完整。现有基于多步扩散的模型计算成本高，难以用于交互应用和边缘设备。

Method: 提出OSOR模型，包含三个创新组件：1）占用引导判别器，用于精确边界监督以实现稳定的单步扩散训练；2）Alpha头，利用预训练扩散模型知识预测合适移除区域，以处理不完美掩码；3）语义锚定验证管道（SAVP），过滤噪声指令三元组以大规模产生效果感知监督。基于SAVP构建了CORNE数据集（280K验证移除对），并标注了AnimeEraseBench和TextEraseBench基准。

Result: 实验表明，OSOR在感知质量上超越了强大的多步扩散基线，同时推理速度快4倍到30倍。

Conclusion: OSOR成功实现了高效、效果感知且掩码鲁棒的对象移除，在保持高质量的同时大幅提升了推理速度，为交互式应用和边缘设备部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Real-world object removal is challenging due to two key difficulties: the target object's non-local effects, such as shadows and reflections, which are difficult to model, and the fact that user-provided masks are often inaccurate or incomplete. With billions of parameters and tens of denoising steps, diffusion-based models achieve strong removal performance at the expense of substantial computational cost, limiting their use in interactive applications and on edge devices. To address these challenges, we present OSOR (One-Step Object Removal), which simultaneously achieves efficient, effect-aware, and mask-robust object removal. Concretely, OSOR introduces: (1) an occupancy-guided discriminator for precise boundary supervision, enabling stable single-step diffusion training; (2) an alpha head that leverages knowledge from pretrained diffusion models to predict appropriate removal regions with minimal overhead, thereby handling imperfect masks; and (3) a semantic-anchored verification pipeline (SAVP) that filters noisy instruction-based triplets to produce effect-aware supervision at scale. Using SAVP, we curate CORNE, which contains 280K verified removal pairs, and further annotate AnimeEraseBench and TextEraseBench to evaluate performance on more complex removal tasks. Experiments show that OSOR surpasses strong multi-step diffusion baselines in perceptual quality while achieving $4\times$ to $30\times$ faster inference.

</details>


### [80] [Cross-view Multimodal Vision-Based Assessment Framework for Traditional Chinese Medicine Rehabilitation Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28104)
*Francis Xiatian Zhang,Hao Yao,Shengxuan Chen,Hong Zhu,Hongxiao Jia,Sisi Zheng,Hubert P. H. Shum*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了CME-AQA框架，通过跨视角、多模态视觉融合技术，解决了中医康复训练（如针灸、推拿）中动作质量评估的难题，显著提升了评估准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自动动作质量评估框架通常依赖于单视角的骨骼数据，难以有效处理中医技术（如针灸或推拿）中常见的密集手部自遮挡和复杂的手物交互问题，导致评估效率低下。因此，需要一种新的框架来应对这些挑战。

Method: 作者提出了一个名为CME-AQA的跨视角、多模态视觉评估框架。该框架整合了视觉-姿态融合技术以增强对环境上下文的理解，并在训练过程中利用第一人称和第三人称视频来提高推理的鲁棒性。为此，研究团队收集了两个双视角数据集：针灸数据集（TCM-AQA61-A）和推拿数据集（TCM-AQA61-T），每个数据集包含61名受试者同步的第一人称和第三人称视频记录及专家标注。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提方法在关键评分任务（如进针深度和快速进针）上，加权F1分数相比最佳竞争方法实现了超过10%的相对提升，总体性能优于或可比拟其他基准方法。同时，在定量指标（如进针时间和操作频率）上降低了平均绝对误差。在CPR数据集上的测试也显示出在多个基于姿势的评估标准上具有可比性能，表明该方法适用于其他以受试者动作为中心的结构化模拟临床技能评估。

Conclusion: CME-AQA框架能够增强结构化中医康复训练的评估准确性，并促进更便捷、更有效的以训练为导向的技能评估。

Abstract: Vision-based assessment can provide convenient and cost-effective evaluation in Traditional Chinese Medicine (TCM) rehabilitation training, where action quality assessment (AQA) from computer vision offers a promising solution. Existing automatic AQA frameworks for physical therapy typically rely on skeletal data captured from a single viewpoint, which is inefficient for TCM techniques such as acupuncture or Tuina that involve dense hand self-occlusion and complex hand-object interactions. To address these challenges, we propose CME-AQA, a cross-view, multimodal vision-based assessment framework that integrates visual-pose fusion to enhance understanding of environmental context and leverages both first-person and third-person videos during training to improve inference robustness. We collected two dual-view datasets, TCM-AQA61-A (Acupuncture) and TCM-AQA61-T (Tuina), each containing synchronized first-person and third-person recordings of 61 subjects with expert annotations. Experimental results show that our approach achieves superior or comparable mean performance against competitive baselines, achieving over 10% relative improvement in weighted F1 over the best competing method on key rating tasks such as Needle Depth and Quick Needle Insertion, while also reducing mean absolute error in quantitative measures such as insertion time and manipulation frequency. Testing on a CPR dataset further demonstrates comparable performance on several posture-based criteria, suggesting applicability to related structured simulated clinical skill assessments where participant motion is central to evaluation. Overall, CME-AQA enhances assessment accuracy for structured TCM rehabilitation training and facilitates more convenient and effective training-oriented skill evaluation.

</details>


### [81] [BiDeMem: Bidirectional Degradation Memory for Explainable Image Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28112)
*Xinrui Wu,Lichen Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出BiDeMem（双向降解记忆），一种用于可解释图像修复的机制。它通过查询检索记忆槽的top-k子集，并利用同一信息支持修复路径和训练时的前向降解解释路径，从而提升修复的语义可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前图像修复领域过度依赖单一的PSNR指标来评估降解感知提示、条件和潜在先验，这对其语义理解的测试较弱。条件信息可能仅起到增加容量、充当全局校正偏差或利用数据集捷径的作用，而未成为真正可解释的降解先验。

Method: 设计了BiDeMem，一个双向降解记忆模块。其核心是：由修复特征和输入统计信息构建查询，从记忆槽中检索一个紧凑的top-k子集。所选的记忆槽身份同时支持推理时的修复路径和仅用于训练的前向降解解释路径。实验在一个受控的多降解NAFNet环境中进行，通过引入新的控制变量来分离不同组件的增益（如校正头、密集查询先验、静态全局先验），并与多种变体进行对比。同时采用了强残差监督和更宽的降解头设计。

Result: 实验结果表明，BiDeMem（BiRank）在PSNR上优于其消融变体（例如，比单独的校正头低0.2588 dB，比密集查询先验低0.2586 dB，比静态全局先验低0.2839 dB）。引入的干预探针显示，BiDeMem在保持修复质量的同时，显著提升了对错误先验和原生先验的敏感性。

Conclusion: BiDeMem通过双向降解记忆机制，不仅是一个有效的图像修复模块，更是一个可证伪的解释机制，能够提供可验证的、与降解相关的语义解释，克服了传统评估中仅关注单一PSNR指标的局限性。

Abstract: Degradation-aware prompts, conditions, and latent priors are increasingly used in image restoration, yet they are usually judged by a single endpoint: whether the restored image obtains higher PSNR. This is a weak test of semantics. A condition can help by adding capacity, acting as a global correction bias, or exploiting dataset shortcuts, without becoming an interpretable degradation prior. We propose BiDeMem, a bidirectional degradation memory for explainable image restoration. A query built from restoration features and input statistics retrieves a compact top-k subset of memory slots. The same selected slot identity supports the restoration path at inference time and a training-only forward-degradation explanation path. The study centers on verifiability in a controlled multi-degradation NAFNet setting. New controls separate the gain from a correction head alone, a dense query prior, and a static global prior: these variants are 0.2588, 0.2586, and 0.2839 dB below BiRank, respectively. Strong residual supervision and a wider degradation head also remain below the full bidirectional memory model. Intervention probes show that BiRank preserves restoration quality while increasing wrong-prior and native-prior sensitivity, framing degradation memory as both a restoration module and a falsifiable explanation mechanism.

</details>


### [82] [Monocular Avatar Reconstruction via Cascaded Diffusion Priors and UV-Space Differentiable Shading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28144)
*Hong Li,Minqi Meng,Yanjun Liang,Chongjie Ye,Houyuan Chen,Weiqing Xiao,Xianda Guo,Guojun Lei,Xuhui Liu,Chaojie Yang,Yanlun Peng,Hao Zhao,Baochang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种数据高效的框架，仅使用不到100个真实3D扫描数据，能够从单张野生图像重建高保真、可重光照的4K分辨率PBR（基于物理的渲染）3D头像。该框架利用预训练扩散模型的先验知识，通过级联的LoRA（低秩适应）模块顺序完成纹理补全、去光照和材质分解，并引入UV空间可微BRDF着色损失以确保物理合理性。实验表明，其结果在真实感和泛化性上优于现有方法，且作者承诺在论文被接受后公开所有代码和模型权重。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从单张野生图像重建高质量3D头像是一个具有挑战性的病态问题，主要障碍在于缺乏高质量的PBR数据以及难以将光照效果与内在材质属性进行解耦。现有方法通常依赖于零散的处理流程或庞大的专有数据集，限制了其效果和泛化能力。

Method: 1. 整体框架：提出一个数据高效的流程，利用统一的预训练扩散模型骨干网络，通过级联的LoRA适配器在UV空间中依次处理三个子任务。2. 具体步骤：a. 纹理补全：使用一个“Inpainting LoRA”模块，利用扩散模型的语义理解能力，补全因遮挡而缺失的UV纹理，生成语义和光度上连贯的细节。b. 去光照与材质合成：引入一个“Light-Homogenization LoRA”模块和一种新颖的“Cross-Intrinsic Attention”机制，协同去除图像中固有的光照效果，并合成像素对齐的PBR贴图（包括Albedo、Normal、Roughness、Specular和Displacement）。c. 物理约束：在材质分解阶段施加一个UV空间的可微BRDF着色损失，迫使生成过程遵守渲染方程，避免基于光栅化监督可能产生的伪影。

Result: 1. 数据效率：仅使用少于100个真实3D扫描数据进行训练。2. 输出质量：能够生成全面、4K分辨率的PBR资产。3. 性能优势：在重建结果的真实感（realism）和泛化能力（generalization）方面，均优于现有的最新方法（state-of-the-art methods）。4. 开源承诺：论文被接受后，将发布所有训练代码和模型权重。

Conclusion: 本文成功解决了从单张野生图像重建高质量、可重光照3D头像的难题。通过巧妙地结合预训练扩散模型的强大先验知识与针对UV空间任务设计的级联LoRA适配器，并辅以物理合理的损失约束，该方法在极其有限的数据条件下（<100个样本）实现了卓越的重建效果。这项工作为生成高保真数字人资产提供了一种高效、实用的新途径。

Abstract: Reconstructing high-fidelity, relightable 3D avatars from a single in-the-wild image is a challenging ill-posed problem, primarily hindered by the scarcity of high-quality PBR data and the complexity of disentangling illumination from intrinsic materials. In this paper, we present a data-efficient framework that leverages the robust priors of a unified pre-trained diffusion backbone to sequentially address texture completion, delighting, and material decomposition. Unlike existing methods that rely on fragmented pipelines or extensive proprietary datasets, we utilize cascaded Low-Rank Adaptations (LoRAs) to adapt the strong generative prior of the diffusion model for each sub-task in UV space. Specifically, we first employ an Inpainting LoRA to complete missing UV textures caused by occlusion, leveraging the model's semantic understanding to generate semantically and photometrically coherent details. Subsequently, a Light-Homogenization LoRA and a novel Cross-Intrinsic Attention mechanism are introduced to remove baked-in lighting and collaboratively synthesize pixel-aligned PBR maps (Albedo, Normal, Roughness, Specular, and Displacement). To ensure physical plausibility, we impose a UV-space differentiable BRDF shading loss during the decomposition stage, forcing the generative process to adhere to the rendering equation without the artifacts typical of rasterization-based supervision. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method, trained on fewer than 100 real 3D scans, generates comprehensive, 4K-resolution PBR assets with superior realism and generalization compared to state-of-the-art methods, and all training code and model weights will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [83] [Toward Robust In-Context Segmentation via Concept Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28149)
*Zhigang Chen,Xiawu Zheng,Rongrong Ji*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种概念引导的上下文分割（CG-ICS）新范式，通过从参考图像中提取高级语义概念来提升分割的准确性和鲁棒性，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有上下文分割（ICS）研究主要关注准确性，但忽略了系统鲁棒性——即模型在不同参考图像下能否为同一查询产生稳定的分割结果。这导致模型性能不稳定，泛化能力受限。因此，本研究旨在从鲁棒性视角重新审视ICS，并设计一种更可靠的分割系统。

Method: CG-ICS采用双路径融合策略：1) 文本概念路径：利用多模态大型语言模型（MLLM）从参考图像中推理出候选语义概念，并通过SAM3驱动的评分函数结合树搜索优化来筛选可靠概念；2) 视觉示例路径：通过简单的上下文构建，在查询图像端提供空间定位。最终，将提取的文本概念和视觉示例输入冻结的SAM3主干网络，激活其分割能力。

Result: 在标准ICS基准测试上的大量实验表明，CG-ICS在分割准确性上达到了最先进水平（state-of-the-art），同时显著提升了系统鲁棒性。具体表现为：不同参考选择下分割结果的方差大幅降低，从而构建了更可靠、稳定的上下文分割系统。

Conclusion: CG-ICS通过引入高层语义概念指导分割，有效解决了传统ICS方法对低层视觉匹配的依赖，实现了准确性与鲁棒性的双重提升。该范式证明了利用语义概念增强模型稳定性的有效性，为未来开发更可靠、可泛化的上下文分割系统提供了新方向。

Abstract: In-context segmentation (ICS) requires a model to segment target regions in a query image using only a few reference images and their corresponding masks, without updating any parameters. Despite recent progress, prior ICS studies have largely overlooked a critical aspect: system robustness, ie, whether the model can produce stable segmentation results for the same query under different references. In this work, we revisit ICS from the robustness perspective and introduce a novel paradigm, Concept-Guided In-Context Segmentation (CG-ICS), which performs segmentation by extracting high-level semantic concepts from references rather than relying solely on low-level visual matching. Specifically, CG-ICS introduces a concept reasoning module that uses an MLLM to propose candidates and a SAM3-driven scoring function with tree-search refinement to select reliable textual concepts, together with a parallel visual exemplar route that provides query-side spatial grounding via a simple context construction. Both the textual concept and the visual exemplar are then used to activate the segmentation capability of a frozen SAM3 backbone. Extensive experiments on standard ICS benchmarks demonstrate that CG-ICS not only achieves state-of-the-art accuracy but also substantially improves robustness, yielding a more reliable ICS system with significantly reduced variance across diverse reference choices.

</details>


### [84] [EchoSonar-R: A Multi-View Reasoning-Enabled Model for Disease Classification and Report Generation in Echocardiography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28164)
*Darya Taratynova,Ahmed Aly,Numan Saeed,Mohammad Yaqub*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EchoSonar-R是一个用于超声心动图分析的多视图推理视觉语言模型，能够同时进行多标签疾病分类和报告生成。它结合时空视频编码与结构感知检测器，以提供空间解剖线索，增强可解释性。该模型通过两阶段训练（SFT和GRPO）优化，在多个数据集上显著提升了分类准确率和报告质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有超声心动图AI模型大多专注于提升分类性能，但缺乏明确的诊断推理和空间定位的解剖学证据，这限制了临床医生的信任度。本文旨在开发一个能整合多视图证据、提供可解释推理轨迹的模型，以辅助临床决策并提高医生对AI辅助诊断的信任。

Method: 提出了EchoSonar-R模型，其核心架构包括：1) 一个时空视频编码器，用于处理多视图超声视频；2) 一个结构感知的心脏检测器，用于提供空间定位的解剖学线索。训练分为两个阶段：首先，在推理标注目标上进行监督微调；然后，采用群相对策略优化，利用针对分类和报告生成任务的特定奖励，在统一的强化学习框架内进行联合优化。

Result: 在私有多视图数据集和两个公开基准（包括MIMICEchoQA）上进行了评估。结果表明：在私有数据集上，宏平衡准确率比最强基线提升了17.1%；在MIMICEchoQA上提升了6.1%；GREEN临床保真度评分达到0.800。模型能够生成基于多视图视觉证据的可解释推理轨迹。

Conclusion: EchoSonar-R成功地将多视图推理能力融入超声心动图分析，通过提供空间定位的解剖证据和可解释的推理轨迹，显著提高了疾病分类性能和临床报告生成的质量。该模型增强了AI辅助诊断的可解释性和可信度，为临床决策提供了更可靠的支持。

Abstract: Echocardiography is the most widely used non-invasive cardiac imaging modality, providing essential information for cardiovascular diagnosis. Interpreting an echocardiogram requires synthesizing complementary evidence across multiple heart views to identify abnormalities and produce structured clinical reports. While recent efforts focus on improving classification performance, most models lack explicit diagnostic reasoning and spatially grounded anatomical evidence, limiting clinician trust. We present EchoSonar-R, a multi-view reasoning-enabled vision-language model that jointly performs multi-label disease classification and report generation from echocardiography studies. EchoSonar-R combines a spatiotemporal video encoder with a structure-aware cardiac detector that provides spatially grounded anatomical cues to improve interpretability and clinician trust during cross-view reasoning. EchoSonar-R is trained in two stages: supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on reasoning-annotated targets, followed by Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) with task-specific rewards that jointly align classification and report generation within a unified reinforcement-learning framework. Across a private multi-view dataset and two public benchmarks, EchoSonar-R improves macro balanced accuracy by 17.1% on the private set and 6.1% on MIMICEchoQA over the strongest baseline, achieves a GREEN clinical faithfulness score of 0.800, and produces interpretable reasoning traces grounded in multi-view visual evidence.

</details>


### [85] [HAT-4D: Lifting Monocular Video for 4D Multi-Object Interactions via Human-Agent Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28215)
*Jiaxin Li,Yuxiang Wu,Zhenkai Zhang,Xinrui Shi,Haoyuan Wang,Yichen Zhao,Su Linxiang,Chenyang Yu,Mingyu Zhang,Yifan Ding,Boran Wen,Li Zhang,Ruiyang Liu,Yong-Lu Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了HAT-4D，这是首个用于从单目视频重建多物体3D几何、时间动态和物理交互的智能体框架。它通过整合视觉语言模型与多级人类反馈机制，有效解决了深度歧义和遮挡问题，并创建了新的基准数据集MVOIK-4D。实验表明其性能达到最先进水平，且少量人类反馈即可显著提升重建质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从海量野外单目视频中提取动态4D物体交互，为扩展具身AI和训练视觉语言动作模型提供高效数据收集途径。然而，现有单目4D重建方法主要关注孤立物体，在多物体交互固有的严重遮挡和复杂动态下往往失效。为弥合这一差距，需要能够处理多物体交互的新方法。

Method: 提出了HAT-4D智能体框架。核心方法是整合视觉语言模型与多级人类反馈机制，在3D生成和4D传播过程中高效解决深度歧义和交互诱导的遮挡，从而生成物理上合理的资产，且无需依赖昂贵的多相机设备。该框架还作为可扩展的数据引擎，用于创建新的基准数据集和评估协议。

Result: 1. 性能：HAT-4D在大多数评估指标上达到了最先进的性能，同时保持了有竞争力的语义对齐。2. 消融研究：引入少量人类反馈能显著改善交互重建效果。3. 应用价值：由HAT-4D产生的数据在微调基线模型时能有效提升其性能。4. 贡献：创建了MVOIK-4D，一个用于单目4D交互重建的开放世界基准，以及一个关注物理合理性和时间一致性的多维评估协议。

Conclusion: HAT-4D是首个能够从单目视频中重建多物体4D交互的智能体框架，它通过结合VLM与人类反馈机制，有效解决了现有方法的局限性。该方法不仅达到了最先进的性能，而且证明了少量人类监督在解决复杂3D/4D重建问题中的关键价值。其产出的数据和基准为社区提供了重要资源，推动了相关领域的发展。

Abstract: Extracting dynamic 4D object interactions from massive, in-the-wild monocular videos offers a highly efficient data collection pathway for scaling Embodied AI and training VLAs. However, existing monocular 4D reconstruction methods primarily focus on isolated objects, often failing under the severe occlusions and complex dynamics inherent in multi-object interactions. To bridge this gap, we propose HAT-4D, the first agentic framework designed to reconstruct the 3D geometry, temporal dynamics, and physical interactions of multiple objects from a single video. By integrating VLMs with a multi-level human-in-the-loop feedback mechanism, HAT-4D efficiently resolves depth ambiguities and interaction-induced occlusions during 3D generation and 4D propagation, yielding physically plausible assets without relying on expensive multicamera rigs. As a scalable data engine, HAT-4D facilitates the creation of MVOIK-4D, an open-world benchmark for monocular 4D interaction reconstruction, accompanied by a novel multi-dimensional evaluation protocol focused on physical plausibility and temporal consistency. Extensive experiments demonstrate that HAT-4D achieves SOTA performance on most evaluation metrics, while maintaining competitive semantic alignment. Ablation studies show that introducing a small amount of human feedback improves interaction reconstruction. Moreover, the data produced by HAT-4D effectively improves baseline performance when used for fine-tuning. Our data and code are available at https://lijiaxin0111.github.io/HAT4D/

</details>


### [86] [Exposure Bias Can Alleviate Itself via Directional and Frequency Rectification in Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28226)
*Guanbo Huang,Jingjia Mao,Fanding Huang,Fengkai Liu,Xiangyang Luo,Yaoyuan Liang,Jiasheng Lu,Xiaoe Wang,Pei Liu,Ruiliu Fu,Ruqi Huang,Shao-Lun Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DEFAR框架通过利用Flow Matching中曝光偏差本身固有的动态信号，实现方向性和频率自适应的自我校正，显著提升了生成模型的性能和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Flow Matching模型存在曝光偏差问题，其根本原因是训练与推理过程存在差异。现有缓解方法主要依赖静态约束或外部启发式规则，效果有限。作者认为，曝光偏差本身包含可用于指导其自身校正的动态信号，但这一点尚未被充分利用。

Method: 本文提出DEFAR（方向频率自适应校正）框架。该框架在训练过程中模拟单步推理以识别曝光偏差，并利用偏差产生的方向性和频率自适应反馈信号来增强模型的抗偏能力。它包含两个核心组件：1) 反向漂移校正（ADR）：将推理时的漂移视为一种信号，训练模型学习如何将偏离的目标状态引导回正确方向，赋予模型内在的主动自我校正能力。2) 频率补偿（FC）：经验观察发现，累积偏差常源于高噪声阶段低频成分的缺失，而曝光偏差本身携带了这些缺失的频率信息。FC利用偏差作为自反馈权重因子，来强化这些缺失的频率成分。

Result: 在CIFAR-10、CelebA-64和ImageNet-256/512数据集上的实验表明，DEFAR在生成质量上超越了先前的基线方法。此外，该方法展现出良好的可扩展性、与其他技术的兼容性，以及更强的推理鲁棒性。

Conclusion: DEFAR通过创新地利用曝光偏差中蕴含的内在动态信号进行自我引导和校正，有效解决了Flow Matching中的曝光偏差问题。这不仅提升了模型的最终生成性能，也为改善生成模型的训练-推理一致性提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Flow Matching (FM) has achieved remarkable generative performance, yet it suffers from exposure bias due to discrepancies between training and inference. Existing mitigation strategies typically rely on static constraints or external heuristics. In this work, we propose that exposure bias itself inherently contains dynamic signals that can guide its own rectification. To leverage this, we introduce DEFAR (DirEctional-Frequency Adaptive Rectification). This framework simulates the single-step inference process during training to identify exposure bias. It utilizes directional and frequency-adaptive feedback signals from the bias itself to enhance the model's bias tolerance. It consists of two key components: (1) Anti-Drift Rectification (ADR). ADR treats inference-time drift as a signal to learn the direction to steer deviated states back toward the target. ADR endows the model with intrinsic active self-rectification capabilities; (2) Frequency Compensation (FC). Empirically, we observe that accumulated bias often stems from a lack of low-frequency components in high-noise stages, and exposure bias carries the missing frequency. FC leverages the bias itself as a self-feedback weighting factor to reinforce the missing frequency components. Experiments on CIFAR-10, CelebA-64, and ImageNet-256/512 show that DEFAR outperforms prior baselines and further demonstrates favorable scalability, compatibility, and inference robustness.

</details>


### [87] [RSICCLLM: A Multimodal Large Language Model for Remote Sensing Image Change Captioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28266)
*Yelin Wang,Zijia Song,Shuo Ye,Chuanguang Yang,Miaoyu Wang,Yong Xu,Zhulin An,Yongjun Xu,Zitong Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 首个针对遥感图像变化描述（RSICC）任务的大视觉语言模型（LVLM）后训练框架RSICCLLM，通过创新的数据生成、差异感知微调和双负偏好优化技术，在仅有7B参数的情况下实现了超越更大规模模型的卓越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RSICC方法多依赖传统深度学习架构，有限的模型容量限制了性能。虽然大规模模型后训练技术在通用领域很成功，但由于数据稀缺和需要精细变化理解，直接将其迁移到RSICC任务仍面临挑战。

Method: 1. 提出RSICCLLM框架，这是首个用于RSICC任务的大视觉语言模型后训练框架。
2. 设计数据生成范式，发布了指令数据集RSICI和任务特定RSICC基准。
3. 引入差异感知监督微调，显式提取变化表示，指导模型感知和理解时序差异。
4. 提出双负偏好优化，采用两种互补的负样本构建策略构建偏好数据集RSICP，以进一步提升模型性能。

Result: 大量实验验证了RSICCLLM的优越能力。该模型仅有7B参数，但取得了显著成果，性能超越了规模大得多的模型。

Conclusion: RSICCLLM成功解决了将大规模模型后训练技术应用于RSICC任务的挑战，通过创新的方法和有效的数据策略，在参数量相对较小的情况下实现了卓越的性能，为该领域的研究和应用提供了新的方向。代码和数据集将公开发布。

Abstract: Remote Sensing Image Change Captioning (RSICC) aims to describe changes between bi-temporal remote sensing images and holds significant research and application value. However, most existing methods rely on conventional deep learning architectures, and the limited model capacity constrains performance. Although large-model post-training techniques have achieved great success in general domains, their direct transfer to RSICC remains challenging due to data scarcity and the need for fine-grained change understanding. To address this, we propose RSICCLLM, the first post-training framework for large vision-language models in RSICC. Specifically, we design a data generation paradigm, release the instruction dataset RSICI, and establish a task-specific RSICC benchmark. We further introduce Difference-aware Supervised Fine-tuning to explicitly extract change representations and guide the model in perceiving and understanding temporal differences. In addition, we propose Dual-Negative Preference Optimization (DNPO), which employs two complementary negative-sample construction strategies to construct the preference dataset RSICP and further refine model performance. Extensive experiments validate the superior capability of RSICCLLM, which achieves outstanding results with only 7B parameters, surpassing models of substantially larger scales. The code and dataset will be made publicly available at https://github.com/keaill/RSICCLLM.

</details>


### [88] [Learning Topology-Aware Representations via Test-Time Adaptation for Anomaly Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28268)
*Ali Zia,Usman Ali,Abdul Rehman,Umer Ramzan,Kang Han,Muhammad Faheem,Shahnawaz Qureshi,Wei Xiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为TopoTTA（拓扑测试时自适应）的新框架，将拓扑数据分析中的持久同调工具整合到测试时自适应（TTA）流程中，旨在解决异常分割中现有方法的局限性，提升分割结果的几何和结构一致性，并在多个基准测试中取得显著改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有用于异常分割的测试时自适应方法主要依赖像素级启发式规则（如置信度阈值或熵最小化），这导致模型在噪声和纹理变化下难以保持结构一致性。此外，这些方法通常将异常图视为平坦的强度场，忽略了表征复杂缺陷几何形状的高阶空间关系。

Method: 提出了TopoTTA框架，其核心是将拓扑数据分析中的持续同调集成到TTA流程中。具体方法包括对异常分数图应用多级立方复形过滤，从而推导出鲁棒的拓扑伪标签，并用这些标签指导一个轻量级的测试时分类器，以增强分割质量且无需重新训练主干模型。该方法避免了依赖特定方法的原始分数阈值进行掩码二值化，能够保持连通性，并适用于2D和3D模态。

Result: 在六个标准基准（MVTec AD、VisA、Real-IAD、MVTec 3D-AD、AnomalyShapeNet和MVTec LOCO）上进行的广泛实验表明，与最先进的无监督异常检测和分割方法相比，TopoTTA平均实现了15%的F1分数提升，尤其是在具有复杂几何或结构变化的异常上收益最大。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，将拓扑推理整合到测试时自适应中，为实现结构感知的泛化提供了一条有原则的途径，从而弥合了几何学习与鲁棒自适应之间的差距。

Abstract: Test-time adaptation (TTA) has emerged as a promising paradigm for mitigating distribution shifts in deep models. However, existing TTA approaches for anomaly segmentation remain limited by their reliance on pixel-level heuristics, such as confidence thresholding or entropy minimisation, which fail to preserve structural consistency under noise and texture variation. Moreover, they typically treat anomaly maps as flat intensity fields, ignoring the higher-order spatial relationships that characterise complex defect geometries. We introduce TopoTTA (Topological Test-Time Adaptation), a novel framework that integrates persistent homology, a tool from topological data analysis, into the TTA pipeline to enforce geometric and structural coherence during adaptation. By applying multi-level cubical complex filtration to anomaly score maps, TopoTTA derives robust topological pseudo-labels that guide a lightweight test-time classifier, enhancing segmentation quality without retraining the backbone model. The approach avoids reliance on method-specific raw-score thresholding for mask binarisation, preserves connectivity, and generalises across both 2D and 3D modalities. Extensive experiments across six standard benchmarks (MVTec AD, VisA, Real-IAD, MVTec 3D-AD, AnomalyShapeNet, and MVTec LOCO) demonstrate an average 15% F1 improvement over state-of-the-art unsupervised anomaly detection and segmentation methods, with the largest gains on anomalies exhibiting complex geometric or structural variations. These findings suggest that integrating topological reasoning into test-time adaptation provides a principled route to structure-aware generalisation, bridging the gap between geometric learning and robust adaptation.

</details>


### [89] [StructSplat: Generalizable 3D Gaussian Splatting from Uncalibrated Sparse Views](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28321)
*Jia-Chen Zhao,Beiqi Chen,Xinyang Chen,Guangcong Wang,Liqiang Nie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了StructSplat，一个无需相机参数、直接从非标定图像进行前馈式、可泛化的3D高斯重建框架。它通过结构化表示、像素对齐特征注入、语义先验和相机对齐策略，显著提升了重建质量和泛化能力，在多个基准测试中超越了现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D高斯重建方法存在局限性：要么需要逐场景优化，要么依赖已知相机参数；同时，它们通常将几何与外观信息纠缠于单一网络架构中，这限制了重建精度和跨场景泛化能力。因此，需要一种无需相机标定、能有效解耦不同信息且泛化性强的前馈式重建框架。

Method: 提出一种结构化表示，将几何、语义和纹理线索以明确角色进行组织。具体方法包括：1. 设计像素对齐特征注入机制，实现从2D观测到3D纹理的精确建模；2. 引入语义感知先验以增强全局一致性；3. 提出相机对齐策略，防止信息泄露并提升泛化能力。

Result: 实验表明，该方法在多个挑战性基准上显著优于现有方法。具体地，在DL3DV数据集上达到28.045 PSNR，比AnySplat（22.377）高出5.67 dB；在跨数据集评估中，于ACID和RealEstate10K数据集上分别比AnySplat高出1.94 dB和1.72 dB。

Conclusion: StructSplat通过结构化表示和一系列设计（特征注入、语义先验、相机对齐），成功实现了无需相机参数的高质量、可泛化3D高斯重建，在重建精度和跨数据集泛化能力上均取得了显著提升，为前馈式3D重建提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: We present StructSplat, a feed-forward and generalizable 3D Gaussian reconstruction framework that operates directly on uncalibrated images without requiring camera parameters. Existing methods either rely on per-scene optimization or assume known camera poses, and often entangle geometry and appearance within a unified backbone, limiting reconstruction fidelity and generalization. Our key idea is to adopt a structured representation that organizes geometry, semantic, and texture cues with explicit roles in the reconstruction process. Specifically, we introduce a pixel-aligned feature injection mechanism to enable accurate texture modeling from 2D observations, incorporate semantic-aware priors to improve global consistency, and design a camera alignment strategy to prevent information leakage and improve generalization. Experiments show that our method significantly outperforms prior approaches on challenging benchmarks. On DL3DV, our method achieves 28.045 PSNR, surpassing AnySplat (22.377) by +5.67 dB. In cross-dataset evaluation, our method achieves +1.94 dB over AnySplat on ACID and +1.72 dB on RealEstate10K. Project page: https://structsplat.github.io Code: https://github.com/J-C-Zhao/StructSplat

</details>


### [90] [PerceptionRubrics: Calibrating Multimodal Evaluation to Human Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28322)
*Yana Wei,Hongbo Peng,Yanlin Lai,Liang Zhao,Kangheng Lin,En Yu,Keyu Lv,Han Zhou,Yin Tang,Haodong Li,Mitt Huang,Hangyu Guo,Jianjian Sun,Zheng Ge,Xiangyu Zhang,Daxin Jiang,Vishal M. Patel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出PerceptionRubrics评估框架，通过原子化评分标准和门控评分机制，严格评估视觉感知模型，揭示现有模型在密集约束下的脆弱性及开源与闭源模型间的感知差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准评估分数已饱和，但模型在实际应用中仍表现脆弱，存在评估与真实性能的差距。需要一种更严格、细粒度的评估方法来揭示模型在细粒度视觉事实和严格约束下的真实能力。

Method: 1. 构建包含1,038张信息密集图像和超过12,000个实例特定评分标准的评估框架。2. 通过新颖的“循环同行评审共识流程”构建黄金描述，并提炼为Must-Right（必须正确的基本事实）和Easy-Wrong（容易出错的细节）双流评分系统。3. 实现“门控评分机制”：与线性平均不同，若模型在强制性视觉事实（Must-Right）上失败，则施加严格的二元惩罚。

Result: 通过广泛评估得出三个关键见解：1. 可靠性差距：模型通常能正确验证碎片化的元素，但在满足严格的合取约束（即同时满足多个条件）时失败，暴露了其在密集感知领域的脆弱性。2. 开源-闭源分层：与推理任务的趋势相反，揭示出开源模型与闭源前沿模型之间持续存在约8%的感知能力缺陷。3. 与人类对齐的严格性：所提出的门控指标在评估严格感知保真度方面显著优于传统基准，证明了严格的感知保真度是可靠生成的前提。

Conclusion: 严格的感知保真度是视觉语言模型实现可靠生成的基础前提。PerceptionRubrics框架通过细粒度的原子化审计和门控惩罚机制，有效弥合了传统基准评估与实际模型脆弱性之间的鸿沟，为评估和改进模型的细粒度感知能力提供了更严格、更贴近真实世界的工具。

Abstract: We introduce PerceptionRubrics, a rubric-based evaluation framework that addresses the gap between saturated benchmark scores and real-world brittleness. Shifting evaluation from holistic semantic matching to rigorous atomic auditing, PerceptionRubrics pairs 1,038 information-dense images with over 12,000 instance-specific rubrics. These criteria are derived from golden captions constructed via a novel Circular Peer-Review consensus pipeline and then distilled into a dual-stream system of Must-Right (essential facts) and Easy-Wrong (fine-grained details) rubrics. Crucially, PerceptionRubrics implements a Gated Scoring mechanism: unlike linear averages, failure on mandatory visual facts triggers sharp binary penalties. Extensive evaluation yields critical insights: (1) The Reliability Gap: models often verify fragmented elements correctly yet fail strict conjunctive constraints, exposing brittleness in dense domains; (2) Open-Closed Stratification: contrary to reasoning trends, we reveal a persistent 8% perception deficit between open-source and proprietary frontiers; and (3) Human-Aligned Rigor: our gated metrics substantially out-align conventional benchmarks, validating that strict perceptual fidelity is the prerequisite for reliable generation.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [91] [Support-Constrained RL Enables Real-World Policy Improvement without Real-World Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27475)
*Raymond Yu,William Huey,Mustafa Mukadam,Anusha Nagabandi,Abhishek Gupta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SCORE框架通过将仿真中的强化学习约束在基于真实数据预训练的生成策略支持范围内，实现了机器人操作策略的有效改进和安全部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器人策略在真实世界训练后往往不精确、缓慢且易受扰动。虽然强化学习可改进策略，但直接在真实世界训练成本高昂；而在仿真中训练，无约束的RL会利用接触与动力学差异，产生不安全、无法迁移的行为。常见的正则化方法又可能因过度约束不完美的先验行为而限制改进。

Method: 提出Support-Constrained Off-Domain REinforcement (SCORE) 这一real-to-sim-to-real框架。其核心是通过“流引导”技术，将仿真中的强化学习过程约束在基于真实数据预训练的生成策略所能产生的动作支持集内。该方法从稀疏奖励中学习，避免了蒸馏过程，且基础策略保持不变。

Result: 在8个真实的多指灵巧操作任务中，SCORE将平均成功率从基线策略的37.8%提升至89.9%，显著优于最佳基线（59.5%），并比基础策略少用36.8%的步骤完成任务。

Conclusion: 当策略优化被适当约束时，仿真能够显著提升真实世界的操作策略性能。SCORE引入了一种新的real-to-sim-to-real策略改进范式，有效解决了策略迁移中的安全性与性能提升问题。

Abstract: Robots trained on real world data tend to be imprecise, slow, and brittle to perturbations. Improving these policies with reinforcement learning (RL) is an appealing alternative, but this process often requires expensive training in the real world. Performing policy improvement in simulation instead provides a far cheaper alternative, but unconstrained RL in simulation can exploit contact and dynamics mismatches, resulting in unsafe behaviors that do not transfer to hardware. Common forms of regularization can furthermore limit improvement by overconstraining to an imperfect behavior prior. In this work, we propose Support-Constrained Off-Domain REinforcement (SCORE), a real-to-sim-to-real framework that constrains RL in simulation to the support of a generative policy pretrained on real data. We instantiate this constraint through flow steering, restricting SCORE to actions the base policy can already produce, which ensures transferable behaviors while maximizing policy improvement. Improving a policy with SCORE requires minimal effort: it learns from sparse rewards, avoids distillation, and leaves the base policy untouched. Across eight real-world dexterous multi-fingered robotic manipulation tasks, SCORE improves average success rate from 37.8% to 89.9%, compared to 59.5% for the best baseline, and reaches success in 36.8% fewer steps than the base policy. Ultimately, through extensive experiments and ablations, we show that simulation can substantially improve real-world manipulation policies when policy optimization is appropriately constrained, introducing a new paradigm for real-to-sim-to-real policy improvement. Videos and code are available at https://weirdlabuw.github.io/score/.

</details>


### [92] [AO-ARC: Almost-Surely Asymptotically Optimal Multi-Robot Motion Planning with ARC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27495)
*James D. Motes,Marco Morales,Nancy M. Amato*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AO-ARC是一种即时多机器人运动规划方法，其初始求解速度与最先进的可行性求解器相当，且随着机器人数量增加，比现有即时方法收敛更快、更可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多机器人运动规划（MRMP）的可行性求解器初始求解快但缺乏最优性保证，而即时MRMP方法虽能渐进优化，但在机器人数量增多时收敛慢且不可靠。因此，需要一种能兼顾快速初始解和可靠渐进优化的方法。

Method: AO-ARC基于AO-x元算法，通过迭代调用原始ARC方法处理有界的MRMP实例，并采用makespan成本指标。该方法利用了ARC的自适应耦合/解耦特性，同时保持了AO-x所需的一致成本边界。

Result: 理论分析证明了AO-ARC的渐近最优性。实验评估在不同协调复杂度的2D场景和3D机械臂场景中进行，结果表明AO-ARC在初始求解时间和收敛性能上均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: AO-ARC是一种高效可靠的多机器人运动规划方法，结合了快速初始求解和渐进优化的能力，并具有理论保证，在实际应用中展现出良好的潜力。

Abstract: We present AO-ARC, an anytime multi-robot motion planning (MRMP) method that achieves initial solution times on par with state-of-the-art MRMP feasibility solvers while converging faster and more reliably than existing anytime MRMP methods as the number of robots increases. AO-ARC adapts the AO-x meta-algorithm for converting feasibility solvers into anytime algorithms by iteratively calling the original ARC method on bounded MRMP instances under a makespan cost metric. This exploits the adaptive (de)coupling of ARC while maintaining the consistent cost bound across robot (de)compositions needed for AO-x. We provide theoretical analysis proving the asymptotic optimality properties of AO- ARC and conduct empirical evaluation on a set of 2D scenarios with different levels of coordination complexity and a 3D manipulator scenario representative of real-world applications.

</details>


### [93] [Spacecraft Fiducial Marker for Autonomous Rendezvous, Proximity Operations, and Docking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27566)
*Ravi Kumar Thakur,Matouš Vrba,Martin Saska*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AstraTag是一种专为太空机器人自主在轨操作设计的多尺度递归基准标记，基于Spidron图案的自相似结构实现多尺度检测，并通过薄板样条重扭曲处理曲面附着问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准标记多为单尺度且主要为地面机器人设计，在近距离（特别是交会对接阶段）容易脱离相机视野，而太空操作环境严苛、失败成本高昂，亟需适用于近距离跟踪的可靠视觉参考标记。

Method: 1. 标记设计：采用基于Spidron图案的递归自相似结构，实现多空间尺度检测。  
2. 标识编码：从模板的三角形子区域提取48位签名，并采用广义里德-所罗门编码。  
3. 检测流程：进行基于轮廓的四边形定位、透视归一化和与预计算字典的签名匹配。  
4. 曲面处理：针对曲面附着情况，采用薄板样条重扭曲方法，利用标记内部矩形边界作为几何对应点。

Result: 在航天器模型（包括平面和曲面）上的基准测试显示，AstraTag在曲面上的检测率高于三层Fractal ArUco和AprilTag两种基线标记。

Conclusion: AstraTag为太空机器人操作提供了一种鲁棒的递归标记选项，其多尺度递归结构和针对曲面的处理机制，使其在近距离自主交会对接等关键任务中具有显著优势。

Abstract: Robotic operations in space are challenging due to the harsh environment and the high cost of failure. Fiducial markers provide visual references that aid autonomous rendezvous, proximity operations, and docking for space robots. However, existing fiducial markers are mostly single-scale and largely designed for terrestrial robotics. Such markers leave the camera's field of view at close range, precisely during the proximity and docking phases where reliable tracking is most critical. This paper presents AstraTag, a fiducial marker designed for autonomous on-orbit robotic operations. The marker template is based on a square Spidron pattern whose recursive, self-similar structure enables detection across multiple spatial scales. Marker identification uses a 48-bit signature derived from triangular sub-regions of the template and encoded with a Generalised Reed-Solomon (GRS) code. The detection pipeline performs contour-based quadrilateral localisation, perspective normalisation, and signature matching against a pre-computed dictionary. To handle markers affixed to curved spacecraft surfaces, it incorporates a Thin-Plate Spline (TPS) re-warp fallback that exploits the marker's internal rectangular borders as additional geometric correspondences. We benchmark AstraTag against three-layer Fractal ArUco and AprilTag on spacecraft mockups with flat and curved surfaces. On curved surfaces, AstraTag achieves a higher detection rate than both baselines, offering a robust recursive-marker option for space robotics.

</details>


### [94] [SceneBot: Contact-Prompted General Humanoid Whole Body Tracking with Scene-Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27581)
*Sirui Chen,Shibo Zhao,Zhen Wu,Jiaman Li,Guanya Shi,C. Karen Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了SceneBot，一个统一的运动跟踪框架，通过基于参考动作和逐链接接触标签的条件策略，使人形机器人能够处理自由空间运动、地形穿越和全身操作等任务，并利用后验场景重建方法生成接触丰富的训练数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的人形机器人强化学习策略在自由空间运动中表现优异，但在接触丰富的任务中遇到困难。这是因为纯运动学跟踪无法解决与物体和不平坦地形交互时的物理歧义性。现有方法缺乏处理复杂环境交互的能力，限制了机器人执行需要物理接触的真实世界任务。

Method: 提出了SceneBot框架，其核心方法包括：1）统一的运动跟踪策略，该策略同时基于参考动作和每个链接的接触标签进行条件化，从而显式定义预期的环境交互；2）后验场景重建方法，该方法从重定向的人类动作数据中推断场景交互图，以解决标注交互数据缺乏的问题；3）使用约7.5小时由此方法重建的、富含接触的数据进行策略训练。

Result: 实验结果表明，SceneBot成功地泛化到未见过的动作和环境。它是首个能够无缝统-自由空间行为和接触丰富行为、执行复杂长时程任务（例如搬运箱子上楼梯）的通用框架。该研究确立了接触条件化作为人形控制的强大接口。

Conclusion: SceneBot是一个开创性的通用框架，它通过显式的接触条件化，首次实现了自由空间运动与接触丰富任务行为的统一，解决了传统方法在复杂物理交互中的局限性。所有代码和数据将开源，为相关领域研究提供了新工具和思路。

Abstract: Current humanoid reinforcement-learning policies excel at free-space motions but struggle with contact-rich tasks, as pure kinematic tracking cannot resolve the physical ambiguities of interacting with objects and uneven terrain. To address this, we introduce SceneBot, a unified motion-tracking framework capable of handling freespace locomotion, terrain traversal, and whole-body manipulation. SceneBot conditions a single policy on both reference motions and per-link contact labels, explicitly defining expected environmental interactions. To overcome the lack of annotated interaction data, we propose a hindsight scene reconstruction approach that infers scene-interaction graphs from retargeted human motion. Trained on 7.5 hours of this reconstructed, contact-rich data, SceneBot successfully generalizes to unseen motions and environments. Our results demonstrate that SceneBot is the first general framework to seamlessly unify free-space and contact-rich behaviors executing complex, long-horizon tasks like carrying a box upstairs and establishing contact conditioning as a powerful interface for humanoid control. All code and data will be open-sourced. More demos and information are available at: https://ericcsr.github.io/scenebot/

</details>


### [95] [Learning to Throw: Agile and Accurate Cable-Suspended Payload Delivery with a Quadrotor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27603)
*Yifan Zhai,Elia Raimondi,Yunfan Ren,Ismail Geles,Yannick Armati,Jiaxu Xing,Davide Scaramuzza*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合高保真模型与物理求解器的混合仿真框架，用以训练深度强化学习策略，实现四旋翼无人机对悬挂负载的高敏捷、高精度动态投送。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四旋翼在时间关键任务（如搜救、医疗运输）中运输悬挂负载的能力已受关注，但负载的高动态精准释放是尚未深入探索的挑战。现有基于模型的轨迹优化与跟踪方法因保守的可行性约束、跟踪误差以及柔性绳索动力学的解析建模困难而性能受限。

Method: 提出一个混合仿真框架，耦合高保真解析四旋翼模型与用于复杂绳索和负载交互的物理求解器，通过每一步交换力来获得物理准确的悬挂负载系统模拟。在此仿真环境中训练深度强化学习（RL）策略，以执行向指定目标的敏捷、精准负载投掷。

Result: 所训练的强化学习策略可直接部署到硬件（零样本），在敏捷性-精度权衡上取得突破，相比基于模型的基线方法，着陆误差最多降低50%，投掷时间最多缩短30%。消融研究表明，耦合仿真框架是实现性能提升的关键。此外，同一训练流程可训练出基于视觉观测（而非显式状态估计）的策略，其精度与基于状态的策略相当。

Conclusion: 本研究通过混合仿真与深度强化学习，有效解决了悬挂负载高动态释放的难题，显著提升了投送的敏捷性与精度。研究开源了仿真器，以推动未来空中动态操控领域的研究。

Abstract: Quadrotors offer the agility needed to rapidly transport suspended payloads during time-critical applications, including search-and-rescue and medical delivery. While suspended-payload transport and traversal for these missions are well studied, the highly dynamic targeted release of the payload remains comparatively underexplored. State-of-the-art approaches typically rely on model-based trajectory optimization and tracking; however, these methods often yield sub-optimal performance due to conservative feasibility constraints, tracking errors, and the inherent difficulty of analytically modeling flexible rope dynamics. To overcome these limitations, we propose a hybrid simulation framework that couples a high-fidelity analytical quadrotor model with a physics solver for complex rope and payload interactions. By exchanging forces between the two domains at every step, we obtain a physically accurate simulation of the suspended-payload system. Leveraging this environment, we train a deep reinforcement learning (RL) policy that executes agile, accurate payload throws to designated targets. Deployed zero-shot on hardware, our RL policy pushes the boundary of the agility-accuracy trade-off, outperforming the model-based baseline by reducing the landing error by up to 50% and the throw duration by up to 30%. Ablation studies confirm that the coupled simulation is the key enabler of these gains. We further show that the same pipeline trains a policy driven by visual observations rather than an explicit state estimate, achieving accuracy comparable to that of the state-based policy. To accelerate future research in dynamic aerial manipulation, we open-source the simulator to the community upon acceptance.

</details>


### [96] [Physics-Guided Robotic Radiation Source Localization along Arbitrary Measurement Paths in Unstructured Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27624)
*Hojoon Son,Kai Tan,Fan Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种基于物理信息机器学习的机器人辐射源定位自动化框架，该方法不依赖于特定测量路径，能够精确估计未知环境中的辐射源位置，同时通过并行计算多个模型提高了系统的鲁棒性和精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人辐射源定位方法通常需要机器人接近辐射源以获得精确测量，这增加了机器人受到辐射损伤的风险。此外，专门为辐射源定位设计的路径规划算法限制了机器人在放射性环境中执行多样化任务的灵活性。

Method: 提出了一种基于物理信息机器学习的自动化框架，设计了物理启发模型张量来处理来自未知障碍物的衰减伽马射线通量信号。采用多个模型并行计算以提高定位的鲁棒性和精确性，该框架不依赖于特定的测量路径。在高保真仿真环境中使用蒙特卡罗粒子传输进行评估，并通过物理实验进行验证，同时应用持续学习技术来增强实际部署的适用性。

Result: 该方法在高保真仿真环境中通过蒙特卡罗粒子传输评估，涵盖不同空间尺度、辐射源类型、障碍物材料和几何形状以及机器人轨迹等多种随机化领域。物理实验验证了仿真评估之外配置的有效性。持续学习技术的应用提高了在线机器人辐射源定位系统的实用性。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于物理信息机器学习的机器人辐射源定位方法成功将机器人辐射感知从点通量检测提升到空间智能水平，实现了在未知环境中不依赖特定路径的精确辐射源定位，提高了系统的鲁棒性、安全性和任务灵活性。

Abstract: Using robots to estimate the location of the radiation source is an effective way to improve efficiency and safety. Existing methods focus on planning the robot's path to achieve precise estimation, typically approaching the source. However, approaching the source increases the risk of radiation damage to a robot. In addition, a path-planning algorithm designed solely for radiation source localization (RSL) limits the flexibility of missions that deploy robots into radioactive environments. This study presents an automation framework for robotic RSL that leverages a physics-informed machine learning (PIML) model to precisely estimate the source location, regardless of measurement paths, in unknown environments. Physics-inspired model tensors have been designed for PIML to handle attenuated gamma-ray flux signals from unknown obstacles, and multiple models are computed in parallel to improve the robustness and precision of the RSL. The proposed method is evaluated in high-fidelity simulation environments using Monte Carlo particle transport across diverse randomized domains, including spatial scales, radiation source types, obstacle materials and geometries, and robot trajectories. The method is also validated through physical experiments on configurations that are not included in the simulation-based evaluation. The continuous learning technique is applied in real-robot deployment to enhance the practical applicability of the online robotic RSL system. The proposed method advances robot radiation perception from pointwise flux detection to spatial intelligence.

</details>


### [97] [P-ARC: Exploiting Subproblem Independence for Parallel Multi-Robot Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27625)
*James D. Motes,Marco Morales,Nancy M. Amato*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: P-ARC是一种为多机器人运动规划（MRMP）设计的并行自适应机器人协调方法，它将ARC的三个主要阶段并行化，并结合OR并行多启动策略（OR-P-ARC）。实验表明，在最多128个机器人的场景中，使用16个CPU核心可实现接近4倍的规划加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人运动规划问题计算复杂，需要高效的并行化方法。现有ARC方法虽能分解问题，但仍有并行化潜力。论文旨在通过并行化ARC的各个阶段，进一步提升大规模多机器人团队规划效率。

Method: 提出P-ARC，将ARC的三个阶段（初始个体解决方案、冲突检测、冲突解决）并行化；引入OR并行多启动策略，形成OR-P-ARC混合策略；在2D移动和平面操作器场景（最多128机器人）中评估不同并行策略的影响。

Result: P-ARC能有效分配冲突检测与解决的工作负载；在真实世界启发的大规模Panda多操作臂团队场景中，使用16个CPU核心时，规划时间加速接近4倍；并行策略在不同规模场景中表现出良好的扩展性。

Conclusion: P-ARC和OR-P-ARC并行策略显著提升了多机器人运动规划的效率，尤其适用于大规模团队；并行化充分利用了ARC问题分解带来的独立性；该方法为实时或准实时大规模MRMP应用提供了可行路径。

Abstract: This paper presents Parallel ARC (P-ARC), a parallel variant of the Adaptive Robot Coordination (ARC) approach to multi-robot motion planning (MRMP). P-ARC proposes a parallel variant for each of the three main stages in ARC: initial individual solutions, conflict detection, and conflict resolution, exploiting the independence created by ARC's decomposition of the MRMP problem. Additionally, we employ an OR-parallel multi-start strategy to both ARC and P-ARC, creating a hybrid parallel strategy OR-P-ARC. We evaluate the impact of the different parallel strategies for ARC using a set of scaling 2D mobile and planar manipulator scenarios with up to 128 robots to control for conflicts and work distribution across the stages of ARC. Additionally, we demonstrate planning time speedups approaching 4X over the sequential version for large Panda multi-manipulator teams in real-world inspired scenarios when deploying 16 CPU cores.

</details>


### [98] [Direct Action-Head Injection of A Grounded 3D Point Unlocks Spatial and Task Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27663)
*Shiang-Feng Tsai,Jin-Cheng Jhang,Yen-Ling Tai,Jia-Hong Lai,Shih-Yun Wong,KangTung-Hsu,Yi-Ting Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种轻量级、模型无关的模块，用于增强视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的泛化能力。该模块通过将3D点云表示的接地信号（即物体位置和任务指令）与夹爪的相对位移计算后，以自适应层归一化的方式直接注入到动作头，无需修改VLA骨干网络或预训练流程。实验表明，该方法显著提升了VLA在物体位置变化和任务指令变化下的操作成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在机器人操作中存在两大局限性：空间泛化（物体位置不同于训练集）和任务泛化（熟悉场景配以不同语言指令）。尽管已有方法尝试通过2D像素坐标等空间和任务感知信息来提升策略的鲁棒性，但通过语言提示或视觉提示进行的现有表征学习未能有效解决这些限制。研究动机在于探索如何更有效地将接地信号表示并注入VLA，以解锁其泛化能力。

Method: 作者提出了一种轻量级、模型无关的模块。该方法的核心是：1) 使用3D点云表示接地信号；2) 计算该信号相对于机器人夹爪的相对位移；3) 通过一个简单的两层多层感知机（MLP）计算空间嵌入；4) 利用自适应层归一化（Adaptive Layer Normalization）将生成的空间嵌入直接注入到VLA的动作头中。该模块无需修改原始的VLA骨干网络或预训练流程。

Result: 在LIBERO-PRO基准测试中，该方法显著提升了模型性能。对于GR00T-N1.6模型，在任务扰动下，平均成功率从31.2%提升至77.5%（提升46.3个百分点）；在位置扰动下，从28.1%提升至60.2%（提升32.1个百分点）。对于π0.5模型也取得了可比的改进，证明了该机制与骨干网络无关。这些结果表明，所提出的模块能有效提升VLA模型的空间和任务泛化能力。

Conclusion: 研究的核心结论是：将充分接地（grounding）的信号提升至3D空间表示，并将其直接注入到动作头中，是解锁VLA模型空间泛化和任务泛化能力的关键。这一目标可以通过在预训练骨干网络之上添加一个轻量级模块来实现，而无需复杂的架构修改。该研究为提升机器人基础模型的泛化能力提供了一种简单而有效的技术路径。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models leverage large-scale vision-language pretraining for flexible robot manipulation, yet at test time they remain brittle along two axes: spatial generalization, when object positions differ from those seen during training, and task generalization, when a familiar scene is paired with a different language instruction than the one seen in training. A growing family of methods addresses this brittleness by endowing a policy with the spatial and task-aware information such as 2D pixel-coordinate for object localization and placement. However, we find that existing representation through language prompting or visual prompting does not address the limitations; in contrast, exploiting a 3D point-based representation and feeding it directly to the action head leads to substantial improvements-revealing that how the grounding signal is represented and injected into the VLA is the true game changer. Thus, we propose a lightweight, model-agnostic module that represents the grounding signal in 3D, computes its relative displacement to the gripper, and injects the resulting spatial embedding directly into the action head through adaptive layer normalization. The entire module is a two-layer MLP that requires no changes to the VLA backbone or pretraining pipeline. On LIBERO-PRO, our method improves the average success rate of GR00T-N1.6 from 31.2 to 77.5 points under task perturbation and from 28.1 to 60.2 points under position perturbation (gains of 46.3 and 32.1 points). Comparable gains are achieved for $π_{0.5}$ as well, demonstrating that the mechanism is backbone-agnostic. Together, these results support our central finding: given adequate grounding lifted into 3D, injecting it directly into the action head is what unlocks both spatial and task generalization in VLAs-achievable with nothing more than a lightweight module on top of a pretrained backbone.

</details>


### [99] [CWI: Composite Humanoid Whole-Body Imitation System for Loco-manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27676)
*Wenqi Ge,Junde Guo,Zhen Fu,Shunpeng Yang,Jiayu Chen,Hua Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CWI框架解耦上下半身控制，结合动作捕捉数据和对抗运动先验，实现人形机器人稳定的全身协调控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全身控制方法面临挑战：纯命令采样方法因稀疏奖励和课程设计困难导致运动不自然；模仿全身动作捕捉的方法因数据集不平衡需要大量数据过滤。需要一种能同时利用多样化操作参考并保证稳定运动的方法。

Method: Composite Whole-Body Imitation (CWI)框架解耦动作捕捉数据使用，上半身利用多样化操作参考，下半身通过双判别器AMP在精选行走/深蹲片段上训练。采用多评论家架构减少目标冲突，教师-学生蒸馏产生仅依赖双手姿势和速度/高度命令的全身策略。

Result: 仿真和真实世界部署评估显示CWI具有竞争力的移动操作性能、鲁棒的全身协调能力，实现无需全身动作捕捉设备的实用远程操作。

Conclusion: CWI框架有效解决了人形机器人全身控制中稳定运动与灵活操作的协调问题，展示了在真实机器人上的实用价值。

Abstract: Achieving everyday tasks with humanoid robots requires coordinating stable locomotion with versatile manipulation. However, existing whole-body controllers still face significant challenges. Methods trained solely via command sampling, without motion-capture (MoCap) data, often struggle with sparse rewards and require carefully tuned curricula to converge. This is especially problematic for upper-body control, where the resulting motions deviate from human-like statistics and degrade whole-body coordination. Conversely, approaches that imitate full-body MoCap data suffer from dataset imbalance, as many locomotion trajectories are overly aggressive for stable-locomotion scenarios, necessitating extensive data filtering and augmentation. To address this, we present Composite Whole-Body Imitation (CWI), a framework that decouples the use of MoCap data for upper-body manipulation and lower-body locomotion. This decoupling allows us to exploit the full MoCap dataset of diverse manipulation references, while stable, command-conditioned lower-body locomotion is guided by dual discriminators trained on curated expert-quality walking and squatting clips via an Adversarial Motion Prior (AMP). A multi-critic architecture reduces conflicts among locomotion, manipulation, and motion-style objectives, and a teacher--student distillation stage yields a whole-body policy conditioned only on bimanual hand poses and velocity/height commands. We evaluate CWI through simulation experiments and real-world deployment on a full-size LimX Oli humanoid. The results show competitive loco-manipulation performance, robust whole-body coordination, and practical teleoperation without full-body motion-capture equipment. A project page with supplementary material can be found at https://cwi-ral.github.io/CWI-RAL-Webpage.

</details>


### [100] [DIM-WAM: World-Action Modeling with Diverse Historical Event Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27677)
*Kai Wang,Zhaopeng Gu,Yixiang Chen,Yuan Xu,Qisen Ma,Peng Su,Zhaowen Li,Yan Huang,Liang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出DiM-WAM，一种记忆增强的世界-动作模型，通过整合多尺度历史上下文、局部未来动态和全局任务进度，解决了现有世界-动作模型在长视野机器人操作任务中因依赖短期预测而导致的长期遗忘和全局状态感知不足问题，并在仿真与真实任务中取得了显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的世界-动作模型主要依赖短期历史和短期未来预测，这对于正确执行依赖于早期观察和任务进度的长期任务来说是不够的。这类具有时间依赖性的任务需要有效利用互补的时间信息，包括近期局部上下文、跨阶段历史事件、即时未来动态以及全局任务进度。因此，需要一种能够克服长期遗忘、增强全局任务状态感知能力的方法。

Method: 提出DiM-WAM（记忆增强世界-动作模型）。其核心是集成一个记忆模块：从真实观测中提取紧凑的视觉事件信息；通过基于独立相似性的合并策略更新多个记忆库；读取带有库标识和时间嵌入的长期上下文，以条件化视频和动作的去噪过程。此外，引入进度监督目标，鼓励记忆标记不仅编码已完成的历史事件，还能编码当前任务阶段及其对剩余任务的影响。

Result: 在仿真基准RMBench上，DiM-WAM将平均成功率从LingBot-VA的28.4%提升至69.8%，显著超过了使用显式记忆的Mem-0基线（42.0%）。在四个真实的Franka机械臂操作任务中，DiM-WAM将平均阶段成功率从70.7%提升至91.5%，并将完整任务成功率从52.5%大幅提升至80.0%。

Conclusion: DiM-WAM通过其独特的记忆增强机制，有效整合了多尺度时间信息，显著提升了模型对长视野任务的处理能力，解决了长期遗忘和全局状态感知的难题，在仿真和真实机器人任务中均展现出优越的性能，为复杂机器人操作任务提供了一种有效的解决方案。

Abstract: World-action models have shown promising robot-manipulation performance by jointly predicting future visual states and actions. However, existing methods mainly rely on short-term history and short-horizon future prediction, which is insufficient for long-horizon tasks whose correct execution depends on earlier observations and task progress. Such temporally dependent tasks require effective use of complementary temporal information, including recent local context, cross-stage historical events, immediate future dynamics, and global task progress. To address long-term forgetting and poor awareness of the global task state, we introduce DiM-WAM, a memory-augmented world-action model that integrates multi-scale historical context, local future dynamics, and global task progress. The memory extracts compact visual event information from real observations, updates multiple memory banks through independent similarity-based merging, and then reads the bank-identity- and time-embedded long-term context to condition video and action denoising. A progress-supervision objective further encourages memory tokens to encode not only completed historical events but also the current task stage and its implications for the remaining task. On RMBench, DiM-WAM raises average success from 28.4% with LingBot-VA to 69.8%, exceeding the explicit-memory Mem-0 baseline at 42.0%. On four real-world Franka tasks, it improves average stage success from 70.7% to 91.5% and full-task success from 52.5% to 80.0%. Project page: https://wangkai-casia.github.io/dim-wam/{\texttt{https://wangkai-casia.github.io/dim-wam/}}.

</details>


### [101] [Drop-Then-Recovery: How Redundant Are Vision-Language-Action Models?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27755)
*Guoheng Sun,Kaixi Feng,Shwai He,Xiaochuan Gong,Yexiao He,Ziyao Wang,Zheyu Shen,Wanghao Ye,Ramana Rao Kompella,Gaowen Liu,Ang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究发现视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型中语言骨干存在高度冗余，提出“Drop-Then-Recovery”（DTR）协议和“GateProbe”指标，通过移除transformer块实验表明，标准机器人操作任务中语言组件可大幅精简而不损失性能，甚至可提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型继承自预训练视觉语言模型（VLM），其语言骨干容量远超机器人短指令的实际需求，导致架构冗余。本文旨在探究VLA模型在闭环控制任务中各部分的必要性，特别是语言、视觉和动作组件的实际容量需求。

Method: 引入“Drop-Then-Recovery”（DTR）分析协议：从预训练VLA模型中选择性移除transformer块，再进行微调，以评估被移除部分对下游任务的必要性。为确保干预可靠性，提出“GateProbe”一次性虚拟门灵敏度指标，根据各块对下游动作损失的贡献进行排序。

Result: 在多种VLA架构、操作基准测试及真实工业场景中发现：语言骨干对标准机器人操作任务高度冗余，而视觉和动作路径对移除更不耐受。在LIBERO基准上，移除一半LLM块后，OpenVLA-OFT在相同微调预算下性能从95.0%提升至98.3%；仅保留两个语言块仍可恢复基准水平性能。

Conclusion: 当前VLA基准测试可能对深层语言基础及组合指令理解施加的压力有限。未来VLA架构应更审慎地在语言、视觉和动作组件间分配模型容量，语言骨干可大幅精简以提升效率。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models enable instruction-driven robotic manipulation, but they inherit oversized language backbones from pretrained VLMs whose capacity far exceeds what is needed for short robotic instructions. This raises a basic question: how much of a VLA model is actually necessary for closed-loop control? In this work, we study architectural redundancy in VLA models by using transformer block removal as a controlled intervention. We introduce \textbf{Drop-Then-Recovery (DTR)}, an analysis protocol that removes selected blocks from a pretrained VLA model and then fine-tunes the resulting model to measure whether the removed capacity was necessary for downstream control. To make this intervention reliable, we propose \textbf{GateProbe}, a one-shot virtual-gate sensitivity metric that ranks blocks by their contribution to the downstream action loss. Across multiple VLA architectures, manipulation benchmarks and even real-robot industrial scenarios, we find a strong asymmetry in post-removal recoverability: \ul{\textit{language backbones are highly redundant for standard robotic manipulation tasks, whereas vision and action pathways are substantially less tolerant to removal}}. On LIBERO, removing half of the LLM blocks even improves OpenVLA-OFT from 95.0% to 98.3% under the same downstream fine-tuning budget, and retaining only two language blocks still recovers baseline-level performance. These results suggest that current VLA benchmarks may exert limited pressure on deep language grounding and compositional instruction understanding, and that future VLA architectures should allocate capacity more deliberately across language, vision, and action components. The code is available at https://github.com/s1ghhh/VLADrop.

</details>


### [102] [SpikeVLA: Vision-Language-Action Models with Spiking Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27807)
*Ruiqi Song,Dujun Nie,Siyu Teng,Baiyong Ding,Xiaotong Zhang,Dong Li,Chenming Zhang,Yuchen Li,Hangbin Wu,Long Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了SpikeVLA，一个用于具身导航的脉冲视觉-语言-动作（VLA）架构，旨在解决现有大型Transformer模型在低功耗、实时场景中部署时面临的推理延迟和能耗问题。该架构包含脉冲视觉编码器、多模态脉冲大型语言模型和脉冲动作策略网络三个关键组件，通过脉冲神经网络和事件驱动稀疏性显著降低了能耗和计算成本，同时在导航和机器人控制任务上保持了有竞争力的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型大多基于大规模Transformer构建，导致推理延迟高、能耗大，这限制了它们在低功耗、实时嵌入式场景中的实际部署。因此，研究目标是开发一种能够显著降低能耗和计算成本，同时保持高性能的具身智能架构。
